[2026-07-15 07:55] Kartik Work: Post-Unban Recovery Protocol

Account Stabilization & Cooldown Guidelines

⸻

Immediate Login Hygiene (Before First Login)

1. Change IP Address

Use a clean IP never associated with the account
Recommended: Mobile data / fresh hotspot
Avoid: Any previously used Wi-Fi networks

2. Use a Clean Device

Reset device or use a new one
Clear cache, cookies, and saved sessions
Do not use devices linked to flagged accounts

⸻

Account Security Reset

3. Update Contact Details

Add a new email address
Add a new phone number
Do not reuse previous credentials

4. Modify Account Identity

Slightly change username (add/remove character)
Set account to private for initial period

⸻

Profile & Content Cleanup

5. Remove Bio Link

Keep bio link empty during cooldown period

6. Remove Risky Content

Delete content that may have triggered the ban
Avoid sensitive or borderline material

⸻

Cooldown Behavior (3–4 Weeks Minimum)

7. Content Strategy

Post only safe, neutral content
Use feed posts or carousels only
Avoid reels completely

8. Activity Control

Limit aggressive growth tactics
Maintain normal, low-risk engagement behavior

⸻

Optional Traffic Handling

9. Audience Redirection (If Needed)

Use a single story highlight for soft redirection
Optionally move audience to a secondary account

⸻

Post-Cooldown Transition

10. Gradual Normalization

Reintroduce links cautiously
Use a different or safer link provider
Slowly return to regular posting behavior
